INSTALL WHEEL:
pip install --force-reinstall ./qiskit-aer-main/dist/qiskit_aer-0.18.0-cp312-cp312-linux_x86_64.whl

DO THIS:
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/local/cuda-13.4/lib64:/usr/local/cuda/lib64:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH"

CHECK THIS:
Because you manually installed cuda-toolkit-13-4 via NVIDIA's .deb files for Qiskit Aer, you are compiling against CUDA 13.4. CUDA 13.x requires a very recent NVIDIA proprietary display driver (Driver version >= 550.54.14).

The Fix: Run nvidia-smi and look at the top right corner.

If the CUDA Version listed there is less than 13.4 (or your Driver Version is older than 550.x), Qiskit Aer will fail to connect to the hardware and will throw the No CUDA device available! error.

If this is the case, you must update your host's NVIDIA drivers using Pop!_Shop or sudo apt install nvidia-driver-550 (or newer).

DO THIS:
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/home/pop-os/quantum_env/lib/python3.12/site-packages/cuquantum/lib:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH"

sudo dpkg -i cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.0-595.45.04-1_amd64.deb
sudo cp /var/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local/cuda-*-keyring.gpg /usr/share/keyrings/
sudo apt-get update
sudo apt-get -y install cuda-toolkit-13-2


sudo apt update
sudo apt install python3-venv python3-pip

source ~/quantum_env/bin/activate

SEEMS TO WORK 2026-10-30 22:55:

4. Install the new Wheel

Because we cleared the dist folder in Step 1, only the new, correct wheel will
exist in it.

pip install --force-reinstall ./dist/qiskit_aer-*.whl

5. Set the Correct Runtime Paths and Run

Finally, wipe out the old LD_LIBRARY_PATH that the AI gave you earlier, and
point it exclusively to your new 13.2 libraries and cuquantum.

export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/local/cuda-13.2/lib64:/home/pop-os/quantum_env/lib/python3.12/site-packages/cuquantum/lib:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH"

https://github.com/Qiskit/qiskit-aer/releases/tag/0.18.2

https://github.com/Qiskit/qiskit-aer/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md#building-with-gpu-support

Download Installer for Linux Ubuntu 24.04 x86_64

wget https://developer.download.nvidia.com/compute/cuda/repos/ubuntu2404/x86_64/cuda-ubuntu2404.pin
sudo mv cuda-ubuntu2404.pin /etc/apt/preferences.d/cuda-repository-pin-600
wget https://developer.download.nvidia.com/compute/cuda/13.4.3/local_installers/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-4-local_13.4.3-1_amd64.deb
sudo dpkg -i cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-4-local_13.4.3-1_amd64.deb
sudo cp /var/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-4-local/cuda-*-keyring.gpg /usr/share/keyrings/
sudo apt-get update
sudo apt-get -y install cuda-toolkit-13-4

AER_THRUST_BACKEND=CUDA

python ./setup.py bdist_wheel -- -DAER_THRUST_BACKEND=CUDA --

pip install nvidia-cuda-runtime-cu13 nvidia-cublas-cu13 nvidia-cusolver-cu13 nvidia-cusparse-cu13 cuquantum-cu13

python ./setup.py bdist_wheel -- -DAER_THRUST_BACKEND=CUDA -DAER_PYTHON_CUDA_ROOT=qiskit-aer-venv --
